# Strict Physics Derivation Obligation Map v0

Document ID:
- `STRICT_PHYSICS_DERIVATION_OBLIGATION_MAP_v0`

Purpose:
- Convert the active ToE physics program into derivational obligations only.
- Exclude paper publication, reviewer-facing packaging, submission readiness, and manuscript polish from the active physics lane.
- Make every proof target explicit enough that an attempted closure either discharges a theorem, exposes a retained assumption, or records a blocker.

Non-claim boundary:
- This document does not claim physics completion, canonical master-action promotion, global seam closure, Standard Model recovery, interacting-QFT completion, or empirical validation.
- This document is a strict physics work map, not a release-governance expansion surface.

Governance-manifest boundary:
- These focused gates are not enrolled into `formal/docs/release/GOVERNANCE_TEST_MANIFEST_v1.json` by this map.
- Governance-manifest promotion remains subject to the recovery freeze policy.

Focused validation ladder:
- Default validation order is changed Lean module, direct dependent Lean module, changed focused Python gate, current-target and loop-registry gates, then the relevant doc or inventory gate when public surfaces changed.
- Full `lake build ToeFormal` and full pytest are escalation-only checks, reserved for broad shared-surface changes, contradictory focused results, or an explicit checkpoint/release confidence requirement.
- Historical focused gates protect their own artifact tokens and non-promotion boundaries while reading the moving live-target state from `formal/docs/release/LOOP_CONTROL_REGISTRY_v0.json`; `test_current_target_freshness_gate.py` remains the exact live-target pin.

What is proved:
- In a finite test-field model, if every test variation pairs to zero with the scalar residual, each residual component is zero.
- If a candidate master-action scalar slice has a first variation equal to the finite pairing with a box-plus-mass residual, stationarity implies the bounded free-scalar/Klein-Gordon-class residual equation.
- In the finite quadratic scalar model, the first-variation formula is now derived from the exact action-shift expansion under explicit linearity and discrete integration-by-parts conditions.
- The finite route has explicit Lean objects for variation direction, boundary/integration-by-parts condition, first-variation obligation slice, free scalar residual, and free scalar action.
- The continuum route now has a Lean-facing theorem target with explicit field, variation-family, pairing, action, residual, derivative-at-zero, and residual-equation objects.
- The continuum algebraic first-variation expansion is mechanically checked conditional on linear integration, linear operator, boundary-term vanishing, and residual-separation assumptions.
- The integration-by-parts boundary step is isolated as a named theorem: boundary-term vanishing implies the continuum integration-by-parts identity.
- `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003` is now split into named continuum analytic sub-obligations: boundary-term vanishing, operator-domain closure, residual-separation principle, smoothness/admissible-variation assumptions, and the supporting integration-linearity obligation consumed by the current algebraic route.
- The Blocker 003 split has a Lean readout that keeps Phase 2 unauthorized, reconstructs the existing `ContinuumFirstVariationObligations` bundle from the named sub-obligations, and preserves the boundary model and residual-separation principle through that reconstruction.
- A small conditional analytic discharge is now recorded: a supplied boundary-term sub-obligation is exactly sufficient to reuse the already-landed continuum integration-by-parts theorem.
- A bounded boundary-model surface now formalizes a two-sided Green-identity boundary flux for a compact-support or boundary-decay scalar class.
- The two-sided boundary flux is mechanically proved to vanish when both field traces vanish, and a closed restricted field universe instantiates `BoundaryTermModel` from that compact-support/decay surface.
- The resulting boundary sub-obligation reuses the existing Blocker 003 integration-by-parts theorem, so the boundary lane is now conditionally discharged relative to a supplied Green identity, trace-vanishing class, and closed field universe.
- The scalar kinetic boundary problem is now named in Lean as the selected operator/function-space pair: kinetic operator plus smooth compact-support or boundary-decay class.
- Compact support or boundary decay at this boundary layer is represented by vanishing endpoint traces, and that trace-vanishing class mechanically supplies the zero-trace condition required by the boundary surface.
- The actual Green identity is now isolated as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A_GREEN_IDENTITY_RETAINED`; it is the exact remaining boundary sub-blocker needed to turn the named scalar kinetic boundary problem into a concrete boundary theorem.
- The 003A discharge attempt now states the Green identity over a selected scalar kinetic operator/function-space pair and proves that, if the required concrete assumption bundle is supplied, the retained Green-identity object and integration-by-parts route follow.
- The current formal model does not yet supply that assumption bundle unconditionally. The next retained sub-blockers are now named: `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A1_DIFFERENTIABLE_FUNCTION_SPACE_RETAINED`, `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`, `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A3_INTEGRATION_REGULARITY_RETAINED`, and `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A4_OPERATOR_DOMAIN_CLOSURE_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2 closed-boundary-universe bounded attempt now discharges a concrete zero-trace, zero-operator model. The zero integral and zero operator are linear, every field has zero boundary trace, the closed scalar kinetic boundary universe is constructed, and integration by parts follows through the existing Green-identity route. This is a trivial model only; the nontrivial scalar kinetic closed-boundary theorem remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A_NONTRIVIAL_SCALAR_KINETIC_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`, with outcome `CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_TRIVIAL_ZERO_TRACE_MODEL_DISCHARGED_NONTRIVIAL_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A1 nontrivial closed-boundary attempt now moves one step beyond the all-zero sanity model. It defines the nontrivial closed-boundary requirement shape, constructs an anchored evaluation integral that is linear and nonzero on the constant-one field, proves the field class is not collapsed, and routes the resulting nonzero-integral/zero-operator/zero-trace witness through the closed-boundary and integration-by-parts path. This still does not supply a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, meaningful trace map, or nonzero-operator Green identity; the retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A1_NONTRIVIAL_GREEN_IDENTITY_OR_TRACE_MODEL_RETAINED`, with outcome `NONZERO_INTEGRAL_ZERO_OPERATOR_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_MODEL_DISCHARGED_GREEN_IDENTITY_OR_TRACE_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A2 meaningful-trace attempt now constructs an anchored two-sided trace that is nonzero on the constant-one field and proves the zero-operator Green identity still holds because normal traces are zero. Lean also proves this meaningful trace cannot feed the current full-field closed-boundary universe API: the constant-one field is not trace-vanishing. The closed-universe-compatible meaningful trace route remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A2_MEANINGFUL_TRACE_MODEL_RETAINED`, with outcome `MEANINGFUL_TRACE_MODEL_NONZERO_TRACE_DISCHARGED_CLOSED_UNIVERSE_COMPATIBILITY_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A3 restricted trace-vanishing field-universe attempt now defines the anchored trace-vanishing field class, proves every member has zero left/right endpoint trace, and proves the class excludes `constantOneField`, so it is not the full field universe. Lean records a conditional adapter from an all-fields trace-vanishing assumption into the current `ClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse` API and also proves that all-fields assumption is impossible for the meaningful anchored trace. The restricted-class-to-current-closed-universe bridge remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A3_RESTRICTED_TRACE_VANISHING_FIELD_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`, with outcome `RESTRICTED_TRACE_VANISHING_FIELD_CLASS_CONSTRUCTED_FULL_FIELD_CLOSED_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A4 restricted closed-boundary-universe API now defines a restricted closed scalar kinetic boundary universe over a field class, a restricted boundary-term model, and a restricted integration-by-parts route. It instantiates the API for the anchored trace-vanishing class and proves restricted zero-operator integration by parts, while retaining the bridge into the existing full-field `BoundaryTermModel` and Phase 2 route as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A4_RESTRICTED_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_API_REQUIRED`, with outcome `RESTRICTED_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_API_DEFINED_FULL_FIELD_BRIDGE_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A5 restricted first-variation interface now defines a restricted boundary interface, restricted first-variation obligations, restricted stationarity/separation shapes, and the adapter condition needed to recover the existing full-field `ContinuumFirstVariationObligations`. Lean proves the restricted algebraic action-shift expansion for admitted base/variation pairs and proves the anchored restricted interface cannot supply the full-field adapter. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A5_RESTRICTED_FIRST_VARIATION_INTERFACE_RETAINED`, with outcome `RESTRICTED_FIRST_VARIATION_INTERFACE_DEFINED_ADAPTER_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A6 restricted KG residual route now defines a weak restricted KG conclusion: stationarity against admitted variations gives residual pairing zero against admitted test fields. Lean proves this weak route, records the conditional upgrade from a supplied restricted separation principle, reuses the restricted action-shift theorem, and instantiates the weak route for the anchored zero-field witness. Concrete restricted separation, nonzero scalar kinetic operator, nonzero-operator Green identity, and Phase 2 remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A6_RESTRICTED_KG_RESIDUAL_ROUTE_RETAINED`, with outcome `RESTRICTED_KG_RESIDUAL_ROUTE_WEAK_THEOREM_DISCHARGED_SEPARATION_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A7 restricted separation-principle surface now defines the admitted-residual separation condition needed to upgrade weak restricted KG residual vanishing into a residual equation. Lean proves the conditional upgrade and also proves a negative theorem: the anchored trace-vanishing test class cannot separate an off-anchor spike residual on a point space with an off-anchor point. Concrete restricted separation remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A7_RESTRICTED_SEPARATION_PRINCIPLE_RETAINED`, with outcome `RESTRICTED_SEPARATION_PRINCIPLE_CONDITION_RECORDED_ANCHORED_OBSTRUCTION_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A8 separating test-class candidate surface now names the stronger admitted test class needed after the anchored nonseparation obstruction. Lean proves that a supplied candidate test class feeds the A2A7 admitted restricted separation route and upgrades weak restricted KG residual vanishing when the KG residual is admitted. Concrete test-class construction, nondegenerate pairing or density, residual admissibility, nonzero scalar kinetic Green identity, and Phase 2 remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A8_SEPARATING_TEST_CLASS_RETAINED`, with outcome `SEPARATING_TEST_CLASS_CANDIDATE_SURFACE_RECORDED_CONSTRUCTION_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A9 residual-admissibility surface now states when the actual KG residual of an admitted field remains admitted. Lean splits this into operator-image, mass-term, and addition-closure evidence, proves that supplied residual admissibility closes the A2A8 admission premise, and proves the anchored zero-operator restricted route is residually admissible. Nonzero scalar kinetic residual admissibility and domain/trace closure remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A9_RESIDUAL_ADMISSIBILITY_RETAINED`, with outcome `RESIDUAL_ADMISSIBILITY_CONDITION_RECORDED_ZERO_OPERATOR_ROUTE_DISCHARGED_NONZERO_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A10 nonzero scalar kinetic operator-domain closure surface now states the domain and trace package needed for the nonzero restricted route. Lean proves that a supplied package feeds A2A9 residual-admissibility closure evidence, proves residual admission and residual trace compatibility, and conditionally upgrades restricted stationarity through the A2A8 candidate route. Concrete nonzero scalar kinetic operator closure, Green identity, separating test-class construction, and Phase 2 remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A10_NONZERO_SCALAR_KINETIC_OPERATOR_DOMAIN_CLOSURE_RETAINED`, with outcome `NONZERO_OPERATOR_DOMAIN_CLOSURE_CONDITION_RECORDED_CONCRETE_PROOF_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A11 concrete nonzero operator candidate surface now defines a formal identity-style nonzero operator at the current abstraction level. Lean proves it is nonzero and linear, preserves the anchored trace-vanishing restricted class, supplies the A2A10 domain-closure package, and feeds residual admissibility. This is deliberately not the true scalar Box/Laplacian operator; the true scalar kinetic operator and Green identity remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A11_CONCRETE_NONZERO_SCALAR_KINETIC_OPERATOR_RETAINED`, with outcome `FORMAL_IDENTITY_NONZERO_OPERATOR_CANDIDATE_DISCHARGED_TRUE_SCALAR_KINETIC_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A12 true scalar kinetic operator candidate surface now separates the physical Box/Laplacian route from the A2A11 identity-style candidate. It defines the required true-operator evidence interface, proves that supplied evidence conditionally feeds A2A10 domain closure and A2A9 residual admissibility, and records that the current formal setting still lacks concrete calculus function space, derivative/Laplacian semantics, geometric kinetic data, a true Green identity, and a concrete separating test class. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A12_TRUE_SCALAR_KINETIC_OPERATOR_CANDIDATE_RETAINED`, with outcome `TRUE_SCALAR_KINETIC_OPERATOR_CANDIDATE_INTERFACE_RECORDED_CONSTRUCTION_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A13 spatial Laplacian kinetic candidate surface now chooses the spatial-Laplacian branch of the true scalar kinetic route. It records the concrete spatial function-space, derivative/Laplacian semantics, spatial operator selection, geometric/coordinate data, trace compatibility, domain closure, residual admissibility, and spatial Green-identity obligations. Lean proves that supplied spatial-Laplacian evidence conditionally feeds A2A12, A2A10, and A2A9. The actual Laplacian construction remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A13_SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_KINETIC_CANDIDATE_RETAINED`, with outcome `SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_KINETIC_CANDIDATE_INTERFACE_RECORDED_CONSTRUCTION_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A14 spatial Laplacian Green-identity obligation surface now isolates the integration-by-parts theorem required by the spatial-Laplacian branch. It states the exact `ContinuumPair` identity with the two-sided boundary flux and proves that a supplied obligation combines with the remaining spatial evidence to feed A2A13, A2A12, A2A10, and A2A9. The concrete spatial Green identity remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A14_SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_GREEN_IDENTITY_RETAINED`, with outcome `SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_GREEN_IDENTITY_OBLIGATION_RECORDED_PROOF_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15 spatial Laplacian boundary-flux representation surface now isolates the flux-representation dependency inside the A2A14 Green-identity obligation. It states a raw spatial integration-by-parts boundary term, states the representation theorem equating that raw term with the existing two-sided boundary flux, and proves that supplied raw integration-by-parts plus supplied representation evidence feeds A2A14, A2A13, A2A12, A2A10, and A2A9. The concrete boundary-flux representation remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15_SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_BOUNDARY_FLUX_REPRESENTATION_RETAINED`, with outcome `SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_BOUNDARY_FLUX_REPRESENTATION_RECORDED_GREEN_IDENTITY_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15 retained target is now explicitly split in `ContinuumSpatialLaplacianBoundaryFluxSubblockers.lean` into raw spatial integration by parts, boundary-flux representation, regularity/domain assumptions, trace compatibility, orientation convention, concrete Laplacian construction, and separating test-class sub-blockers. Lean proves the split still feeds the existing A2A15-to-A2A14 conditional route when all evidence is supplied, and it records that the parent A2A15 blocker remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15_SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_BOUNDARY_FLUX_REPRESENTATION_RETAINED`, with retained split outcome `SPATIAL_LAPLACIAN_BOUNDARY_FLUX_SUBBLOCKERS_SPLIT_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A raw spatial IBP proof-contract slice now constructs a finite two-endpoint interval model, a nonzero graph-Laplacian surrogate, linear finite integral/operator evidence, a zero-normal endpoint trace, and a raw zero boundary flux. Lean proves the raw spatial integration-by-parts statement, proves the raw flux is represented by the repo two-sided boundary flux for that finite model, and routes the checked evidence through A2A15 into the A2A14 Green-identity obligation. The analytic interval lift, continuum derivative/Laplacian semantics, nonzero normal-derivative flux, domain regularity, orientation convention, and separating test class remain retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A_RAW_SPATIAL_IBP_PROOF_CONTRACT_RETAINED`, with outcome `FINITE_TWO_POINT_SPATIAL_IBP_MODEL_DISCHARGED_ANALYTIC_INTERVAL_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A1 analytic interval lift contract is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialAnalyticIntervalLift.lean`. It defines the finite-to-continuum interval-lift target for the two-endpoint graph-Laplacian surrogate and states the required convergence channels: graph-Laplacian action to continuum derivative/Laplacian semantics, finite endpoint flux to continuum boundary flux, finite raw IBP to the continuum Green identity, finite pairing convergence, trace-normal-derivative convergence, domain regularity, orientation compatibility, and a separating limit test class. Lean proves that a supplied witness for those retained clauses would recover continuum raw spatial IBP, boundary-flux representation, and the A2A14 Green-identity route. The analytic convergence proof itself remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1_ANALYTIC_INTERVAL_LIFT_RETAINED`, with outcome `A2A15A1_ANALYTIC_INTERVAL_LIFT_CONTRACT_RECORDED_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A1A graph-Laplacian convergence channel split is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianConvergence.lean`. It isolates graph-Laplacian action convergence, continuum second-derivative semantics, continuum Laplacian semantics, graph-Laplacian scaling convention, refinement relation, sample/reconstruction compatibility, operator-domain assumptions, graph-Laplacian consistency, and operator-action convergence mode. Lean proves that supplied evidence for those retained clauses fills the A2A15A1 parent graph-convergence field and, with the remaining A2A15A1 channels supplied, feeds the A2A14 Green-identity route. The actual graph-Laplacian-to-continuum-Laplacian convergence theorem remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1A_GRAPH_LAPLACIAN_TO_CONTINUUM_LAPLACIAN_RETAINED`, with outcome `A2A15A1A_GRAPH_LAPLACIAN_TO_CONTINUUM_LAPLACIAN_CHANNEL_SPLIT_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A1B finite endpoint-flux convergence channel split is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialEndpointFluxConvergence.lean`. It isolates endpoint flux representation, continuum boundary trace semantics, continuum normal-derivative semantics, orientation convention, boundary reconstruction compatibility, flux-term convergence mode, finite endpoint-flux consistency, trace-normal-derivative convergence, and orientation compatibility. Lean proves that supplied evidence for those retained clauses fills the A2A15A1 parent finite-endpoint-flux, trace-normal convergence, and orientation-compatibility fields and, with the remaining A2A15A1 channels supplied, feeds the A2A14 Green-identity route. The actual finite endpoint-flux-to-continuum-boundary-flux convergence theorem remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1B_FINITE_ENDPOINT_FLUX_TO_CONTINUUM_BOUNDARY_FLUX_RETAINED`, with outcome `A2A15A1B_FINITE_ENDPOINT_FLUX_TO_CONTINUUM_BOUNDARY_FLUX_CHANNEL_SPLIT_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A1C finite raw-IBP to continuum Green-identity channel split is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialRawIBPToGreenIdentityConvergence.lean`. It isolates the finite raw IBP statement, continuum Green identity statement, finite-to-continuum transfer rule, A1A graph-Laplacian dependency, A1B endpoint-flux dependency, operator/flux convergence compatibility, pairing compatibility, domain regularity for identity passage, and Green-identity convergence mode. Lean proves that supplied evidence for those retained clauses fills the A2A15A1 parent finite-raw-IBP-to-continuum-Green-identity field and, with A1A/A1B evidence plus remaining lift fields, feeds the A2A14 Green-identity route. The actual finite-raw-IBP-to-continuum-Green-identity convergence theorem remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1C_FINITE_RAW_IBP_TO_CONTINUUM_GREEN_IDENTITY_RETAINED`, with outcome `A2A15A1C_FINITE_RAW_IBP_TO_CONTINUUM_GREEN_IDENTITY_CHANNEL_SPLIT_RETAINED`.
- The 003A2A15A1 analytic interval-lift channel assembly/readout is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialAnalyticIntervalLiftAssembly.lean`. It records `ANALYTIC_INTERVAL_LIFT_CHANNELS_SPLIT_COMPLETE_BUT_RETAINED`: A1A graph-Laplacian convergence, A1B endpoint-flux convergence, and A1C raw-IBP/Green-identity convergence are all structurally represented and retained; A2A15A1 and A2A15 remain retained; Phase 2 remains unauthorized. The readout pins the post-capstone anti-loop rule `A2A15A1_NO_MORE_CHILD_SPLITS_WITHOUT_PROOF_FALSIFICATION_OR_CONCRETE_OBSTRUCTION` and names A1A graph-Laplacian-to-continuum-Laplacian as the next theorem-producing target.
- The 003A2A15A1A theorem-facing quadratic-stencil successor is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianQuadraticConsistency.lean`. Lean proves exact centered three-point graph-Laplacian consistency for quadratic samples: the unscaled numerator is `2 * a * h * h`, and the scaled value equals the quadratic continuum second derivative under nonzero spacing. It records outcome `A2A15A1A_QUADRATIC_STENCIL_CONSISTENCY_PROVED_CONCRETE_OBSTRUCTION_RECORDED` and concrete obstructions: only quadratic samples, only one interior stencil, no general function space, no refinement-family limit, no sample/reconstruction compatibility, no uniform or operator-norm convergence, no continuum Laplacian semantics, and no operator-domain closure. The parent A1A channel remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1A_GRAPH_LAPLACIAN_TO_CONTINUUM_LAPLACIAN_RETAINED`; Phase 2 remains unauthorized.
- The 003A2A15A1A stencil-remainder successor is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianStencilRemainder.lean`. Lean proves the symmetric centered stencil cancels the odd cubic contribution, proves the quadratic-plus-cubic-plus-remainder numerator is the quadratic numerator plus the centered remainder numerator, and proves the exact scaled error identity. It records outcome `A2A15A1A_STENCIL_REMAINDER_ERROR_BOUND_RECORDED`; the bound itself is a supplied scale-normalized remainder condition, not a derived Taylor theorem. Remaining obstructions include local three-point scope, assumed-not-derived remainder bound, no Taylor remainder theorem, no uniform remainder control, no refinement-family limit, no general function space, no sample/reconstruction compatibility, no continuum Laplacian semantics, and no operator-domain closure. The parent A1A channel and Phase 2 remain retained.
- The 003A2A15A1A2 Taylor/remainder-control surface is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianTaylorRemainderControl.lean`. It defines the required control object: differentiability order at least four, bounded fourth-derivative or equivalent smoothness evidence, a fourth-derivative bound, local interval model, mesh scale matching the stencil spacing, refinement parameter, refinement-scale compatibility, and the scale-normalized remainder bound. Lean proves that supplied control gives the bound required by the stencil-remainder theorem and conditionally feeds the local stencil error bound. The actual Taylor/remainder-control theorem remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1A2_TAYLOR_REMAINDER_CONTROL_RETAINED`; no Taylor theorem, uniform refinement theorem, full A1A convergence, or Phase 2 authorization is claimed.
- The 003A2A15A1A3 fourth-derivative-bound bridge is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianFourthDerivativeRemainder.lean`. It defines the certificate required to derive stencil remainder control from a fourth-derivative bound: differentiability order at least four, nonnegative fourth-derivative bound, bounded-fourth-derivative-on-interval evidence, local interval model, local Taylor formula, fourth-order remainder formula, centered stencil residual estimate, mesh scale matching spacing, refinement parameter, and refinement uniformity condition. Lean proves that such a supplied certificate gives the prior scale-normalized bound, constructs `TaylorRemainderControl`, and conditionally feeds the local stencil error theorem. The actual derivation remains retained as `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A15A1A3_FOURTH_DERIVATIVE_BOUND_TO_STENCIL_REMAINDER_RETAINED`; no concrete fourth-derivative theorem, full A1A convergence, or Phase 2 authorization is claimed.
- The 003A2A15A1A4 degree <= 3 polynomial certificate is now explicit in `ContinuumSpatialGraphLaplacianPolynomialFourthDerivativeCertificate.lean`. Lean proves the zero polynomial remainder supplies the scale-normalized stencil remainder bound with zero fourth-derivative tolerance, constructs the A1A3 fourth-derivative certificate for degree <= 3 polynomial samples, constructs the prior `TaylorRemainderControl`, and conditionally feeds the local stencil error theorem. ## Continuum Assumption Inventory

The continuum lift is currently conditional on the following named Lean objects:

| Object | Meaning | Current status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `ContinuumAssumptionInventory` | Declares smoothness class, boundary decay/compact support, admissible variations, operator domain, and mass/operator sign convention. | Retained assumption inventory. |
| `ContinuumAssumptionWitness` | Records that a chosen field and variation satisfy the inventory. | Retained per-field witness obligation. |
| `LinearIntegral` | Integral is linear under addition and scalar multiplication. | Retained analytic assumption. |
| `LinearOperator` | Continuum kinetic/residual operator is linear. | Retained operator-domain assumption. |
| `BoundaryTermModel` | Integration by parts holds with a boundary term and that boundary term vanishes. | Boundary bottleneck isolated. |
| `SeparationPrinciple` | If all variation pairings with a residual vanish, the residual vanishes. | Retained functional-analytic assumption. |

`PHASE1-BLOCKER-003` is now represented by `ContinuumAnalyticBlocker003.lean`, which splits the retained analytic debt into the following named sub-obligations:
- boundary-term vanishing,
- operator-domain closure,
- residual-separation principle,
- smoothness/admissible-variation assumptions,
- integration-linearity support obligation.

The split is an adjudication and routing surface only. It preserves the current Phase 2 hold and does not claim analytic closure.

The first boundary-specific increment is represented by `ContinuumBoundaryTermModel.lean`. It introduces:
- `TwoSidedBoundaryTrace`,
- `twoSidedBoundaryFlux`,
- `TwoSidedCompactSupportDecayBoundarySurface`,
- `ClosedCompactSupportDecayFieldUniverse`,
- `boundaryTermModelOfCompactSupportDecaySurface`.

This proves that trace vanishing for a compact-support or boundary-decay class kills the two-sided boundary flux and yields a `BoundaryTermModel` over a closed restricted field universe. It remains conditional on supplying the actual Green identity and the actual closed function-space universe.

The named scalar-operator/function-space slice is represented by `ContinuumGreenIdentityRetained.lean`. It introduces:
- `ScalarBoundaryOperatorKind.kineticBox`,
- `ScalarBoundaryFunctionSpaceKind.smoothCompactSupportOrDecay`,
- `ScalarKineticBoundaryProblem`,
- `TraceVanishingCompactSupportOrDecay`,
- `Phase1Blocker003AGreenIdentityRetained`,
- `ClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse`.

This proves the easy boundary step for the selected slice: trace-vanishing compact-support/decay data supplies zero endpoint traces and, if the retained scalar kinetic Green identity and closed restricted universe are supplied, the selected scalar kinetic route gives the integration-by-parts identity. The retained sub-blocker is now exactly `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A_GREEN_IDENTITY_RETAINED`.

The 003A discharge attempt is represented by `ContinuumGreenIdentityAttempt.lean`. It introduces:
- `ScalarKineticOperatorFunctionSpacePair`,
- `ScalarKineticGreenIdentityStatement`,
- `ScalarKineticGreenIdentityAssumptionBundle`,
- `Phase1Blocker003AGreenIdentitySubBlocker`.

This states the scalar kinetic Green identity over the selected pair and proves that the assumption bundle yields the retained Green-identity object and integration-by-parts theorem. Because the current formal model does not yet construct that bundle from calculus, the next retained assumptions are split into concrete differentiable function space, closed boundary universe, integration regularity, and operator-domain closure.

The 003A2 closed-boundary-universe bounded discharge attempt is represented by `ContinuumClosedBoundaryUniverseDischargeAttempt.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2ANontrivialClosedBoundaryUniverseRetainedId`,
- `closedBoundaryUniverseTrivialModelOutcomeId`,
- `Phase1Blocker003A2ANontrivialClosedBoundaryMissingObject`,
- `zeroContinuumIntegral`,
- `zeroKineticOperator`,
- `zeroTwoSidedBoundaryTrace`,
- `trivialClosedBoundaryPair`,
- `trivialClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse`,
- `trivialScalarKineticGreenIdentityAssumptionBundle`,
- `trivialClosedBoundaryTermModel`,
- `ClosedBoundaryUniverseDischargeAttemptStatus`.

This slice gives a mechanically checked closed-boundary model, but only in the trivial zero-integral, zero-operator, zero-trace regime. Lean proves zero integral linearity, zero operator linearity, trace vanishing for every field, the closed boundary universe, the Green-identity statement, the assumption-bundle route, and the resulting integration-by-parts identity. It deliberately does not claim a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a real continuum integral, a real smooth compact-support field class, a trace-existence theorem, a nontrivial scalar kinetic Green identity, operator-domain closure, residual separation, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A_NONTRIVIAL_SCALAR_KINETIC_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`.

The 003A2A1 nontrivial closed-boundary attempt is represented by `ContinuumNontrivialClosedBoundaryUniverseAttempt.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2A1NontrivialGreenIdentityOrTraceModelRetainedId`,
- `nonzeroIntegralZeroOperatorClosedBoundaryOutcomeId`,
- `Phase1Blocker003A2A1MissingObject`,
- `NontrivialClosedBoundaryUniverseRequirementShape`,
- `anchoredContinuumIntegral`,
- `anchoredZeroOperatorClosedBoundaryPair`,
- `anchoredZeroClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse`,
- `anchoredZeroScalarKineticGreenIdentityAssumptionBundle`,
- `anchoredZeroClosedBoundaryTermModel`,
- `NontrivialClosedBoundaryUniverseAttemptStatus`.

This slice defines the requirements for a genuine nontrivial closed-boundary route: a nonzero integral or operator, a meaningful trace model, a noncollapsed field class, and a Green identity or trace model for the nontrivial setting. Lean discharges only the smallest safe witness: the anchored evaluation integral is linear and nonzero, the constant-one field proves the field class is not collapsed, the zero operator and zero trace still give a closed boundary universe, and the existing Green-identity and integration-by-parts route applies for that zero-operator witness. It deliberately does not claim a meaningful trace map, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, residual separation, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A1_NONTRIVIAL_GREEN_IDENTITY_OR_TRACE_MODEL_RETAINED`.

The 003A2A2 meaningful-trace attempt is represented by `ContinuumMeaningfulTraceModelAttempt.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2A2MeaningfulTraceModelRetainedId`,
- `meaningfulTraceModelAttemptOutcomeId`,
- `Phase1Blocker003A2A2MissingObject`,
- `MeaningfulTwoSidedTraceModel`,
- `anchoredMeaningfulTwoSidedBoundaryTrace`,
- `anchoredMeaningfulTraceModel`,
- `anchoredTraceZeroOperatorClosedBoundaryPair`,
- `anchoredTraceZeroOperatorBoundaryProblem`,
- `AnchoredMeaningfulTraceFeedsClosedBoundaryUniverse`,
- `MeaningfulTraceModelAttemptStatus`.

This slice proves the anchored trace is meaningful by giving a field whose left and right endpoint traces are one. It keeps normal-derivative traces zero, so the two-sided boundary flux vanishes and the zero-operator Green identity remains mechanically checked. The slice also proves the obstruction that prevents using this trace with the current closed-boundary-universe API: because `ClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse` quantifies over every continuum field, the constant-one field would need to be trace-vanishing, contradicting the meaningful trace witness. It deliberately does not claim a closed-universe-compatible trace class, restricted trace-vanishing field universe, nonzero scalar kinetic operator, Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, residual separation, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A2_MEANINGFUL_TRACE_MODEL_RETAINED`.

The 003A2A3 restricted trace-vanishing field-universe attempt is represented by `ContinuumRestrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverse.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2A3RestrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverseRetainedId`,
- `restrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverseOutcomeId`,
- `Phase1Blocker003A2A3MissingObject`,
- `AnchoredTraceVanishingFieldClass`,
- `RestrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverse`,
- `anchoredRestrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverse`,
- `closedBoundaryUniverseOfFullFieldTraceVanishingAssumption`,
- `RestrictedTraceVanishingFieldUniverseAttemptStatus`.

This slice defines the restricted field class exactly as fields whose anchored left/right endpoint traces vanish. Lean proves members of the class have zero traces, `constantOneField` is excluded, and therefore the restricted class is not the full field universe. The existing full-field `ClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse` API can be built only under the stronger assumption that every field belongs to this restricted class; Lean records that conditional adapter and separately proves the all-fields assumption is impossible for the meaningful anchored trace. It deliberately does not claim an API bridge from a restricted class into the existing full-field closed universe, a closed universe over a restricted field type, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, residual separation, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A3_RESTRICTED_TRACE_VANISHING_FIELD_UNIVERSE_RETAINED`.

The 003A2A4 restricted closed-boundary-universe API is represented by `ContinuumRestrictedClosedBoundaryUniverseAPI.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2A4RestrictedClosedBoundaryUniverseApiRequiredId`,
- `restrictedClosedBoundaryUniverseApiOutcomeId`,
- `Phase1Blocker003A2A4MissingObject`,
- `RestrictedClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse`,
- `RestrictedScalarKineticGreenIdentityStatement`,
- `RestrictedBoundaryTermModel`,
- `restrictedBoundaryTermModelOfRestrictedClosedUniverse`,
- `fullClosedUniverseOfRestrictedClosedUniverseAndAllFields`,
- `anchoredRestrictedClosedScalarKineticBoundaryUniverse`,
- `anchoredRestrictedBoundaryTermModel`,
- `RestrictedClosedBoundaryUniverseApiAttemptStatus`.

This slice defines the restricted boundary API that 003A2A3 showed was missing. Lean proves that a restricted closed universe plus restricted Green identity builds a restricted boundary-term model, that this model suffices for restricted integration by parts, and that the anchored trace-vanishing class instantiates the restricted zero-operator route. It also records the adapter into the existing full-field universe only under an all-fields-in-class assumption. It deliberately does not claim a bridge from the restricted API into the current full-field `BoundaryTermModel` or first-variation route, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, residual separation, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A4_RESTRICTED_CLOSED_BOUNDARY_UNIVERSE_API_REQUIRED`.

The 003A2A5 restricted first-variation interface is represented by `ContinuumRestrictedFirstVariationInterface.lean`. It introduces:
- `phase1Blocker003A2A5RestrictedFirstVariationInterfaceRetainedId`,

This slice proves a restricted version of the algebraic action-shift theorem: if the base field and variation lie in the restricted boundary model's field class, restricted integration by parts suffices for the kinetic expansion and the first-variation action-shift formula. It also proves the existing full-field theorem can be recovered from the restricted interface only after supplying an all-fields-in-class adapter, and the anchored meaningful-trace restricted class cannot supply that adapter. It deliberately does not claim a full-field `BoundaryTermModel`, a fully closed first-variation route, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, residual separation without a supplied restricted principle, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A5_RESTRICTED_FIRST_VARIATION_INTERFACE_RETAINED`.

This slice turns the restricted first-variation interface into a bounded theorem route without closing the unrestricted continuum theorem. Lean proves that restricted stationarity implies weak restricted KG residual vanishing, meaning residual pairing zero against admitted test fields. It also proves that a supplied restricted separation principle conditionally upgrades the weak result to the old residual equation, reuses the restricted action-shift expansion from 003A2A5, and gives an anchored zero-field weak witness. It deliberately does not claim a concrete restricted separation principle, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, a nontrivial restricted stationary point, full-field route recovery, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A6_RESTRICTED_KG_RESIDUAL_ROUTE_RETAINED`.

This slice records the exact separation condition needed by the weak restricted KG route. It proves that admitted restricted separation upgrades weak KG residual vanishing into a residual equation when the residual is admitted, and that the stronger pre-existing `RestrictedSeparationPrinciple` implies this admitted-residual version. It also proves the anchored trace-vanishing class is not a valid concrete separating test class: an off-anchor spike is admitted, nonzero, and invisible to all anchored admitted tests. It deliberately does not claim a concrete separating test class, residual admissibility for real KG residuals, nondegenerate continuum pairing, a nonzero scalar kinetic operator, a Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, full-field route recovery, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A7_RESTRICTED_SEPARATION_PRINCIPLE_RETAINED`.

This slice names the replacement route after the anchored trace-vanishing class is shown nonseparating. A candidate package contains a selected test class, proof that each selected test is admitted by the restricted field class, and a theorem that vanishing against those tests separates admitted residuals. Lean proves that a supplied candidate feeds the A2A7 admitted restricted separation principle, upgrades weak restricted KG residual vanishing when residual admissibility is supplied, and recovers the stronger restricted separation principle only under an explicit all-residuals-admitted hypothesis. It deliberately does not claim a concrete separating test class, density or duality theorem, nondegenerate continuum pairing, residual admissibility for real KG residuals, nonzero scalar kinetic operator, Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, full-field route recovery, or Phase 2 authorization. This slice states the residual-admissibility premise needed by A2A8: if a base field is admitted, then its KG residual is admitted by the same restricted class. Lean decomposes that premise into operator-image closure, mass-term closure, and addition closure, proves that supplied residual admissibility lets a separating test-class candidate upgrade weak restricted KG residual vanishing to the old residual equation for admitted fields, and proves a bounded zero-operator result for the anchored restricted class. The anchored trace-vanishing class is closed under scalar multiplication and pointwise addition, so the anchored zero-operator route is residually admissible. It deliberately does not claim nonzero scalar kinetic residual admissibility, nonzero operator domain closure, residual boundary-trace compatibility, concrete separating test-class construction, nondegenerate pairing or density, full-field route recovery, Green identity for a nonzero operator, operator-domain closure, or Phase 2 authorization. The retained blocker is `PHASE1-BLOCKER-003A2A9_RESIDUAL_ADMISSIBILITY_RETAINED`.

This slice tests the smallest nonzero operator candidate supportable at the current abstraction level: the identity operator on continuum fields. It deliberately does not claim this formal identity operator is the physical scalar Box/Laplacian, does not construct a concrete calculus function space, does not prove the true operator mapping or trace theorem, does not prove a Green identity for the true nonzero operator, does not construct a concrete separating test class, does not recover the full-field route, and does not authorize Phase 2. ## Derivation-Grade Standard

A result counts as derivational only when it supplies:
- starting assumptions,
- mathematical object definitions,
- variational or dynamical equation,
- regime or limit map,
- equivalence or approximation theorem,
- explicit residual/error term when approximate,
- proof or mechanically checked formal counterpart.

A result does not count as derivational if it only supplies:
- artifact presence,
- schema consistency,
- status alignment,
- scaffold tokens,
- assumption-preserving restatement,
- publication or submission readiness.

## Cross-Pillar Frontier Sweep

Protocol surface:
- Lean surface: `formal/toe_formal/ToeFormal/Derivation/CrossPillarDerivationProtocol.lean`
- Standard method: `target -> evidence package -> conditional bridge -> obstruction/counterexample -> retained blocker -> next strict target`.
- Uniform statuses: `proved`, `conditional`, `retained`, `refuted`, `not_authorized`.

Frontier map surface:
- Lean surface: `formal/toe_formal/ToeFormal/Derivation/CrossPillarClosureFrontier.lean`
- Rows: Scalar/QFT, QM evolution, QM-STAT, SR covariance, GR01, Cosmology, QFT-GR seam, GR-QM seam, EM-QFT seam, master action.
- Each row records strongest surface, retained blocker, proof-debt scope, master-action dependency, and next strict slice.

Master-action dependency frontier:
- Lean surface: `formal/toe_formal/ToeFormal/Derivation/MasterActionDependencyFrontier.lean`
- Dependency classes: `required_for_coherence`, `required_for_closure`, `publication_grade_only`, `local_proof_debt`.
- Output is citation-boundary only: the master action may cite retained assumptions under their listed scope boundaries, but this map does not promote the master action.
- QM-STAT citation scope now includes the component residual evidence under supplied finite alignment; the dependency class remains `required_for_coherence` and does not promote the master action.
